an unserious person entertaining a group."],"tags":["figuratively"]},{"examples":[{"text":"There was some kind of fiddle going on at that company, and several of the directors were arrested.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Unskillful or unartful behavior, particularly when showy and superficially pleasing.","A scam; a fraud or swindle."],"tags":["figuratively","informal"]},{"examples":[{"text":"That parameter setting is just a fiddle to make the lighting look right.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Unskillful or unartful behavior, particularly when showy and superficially pleasing.","A workaround; a quick and less than perfect solution for some flaw or problem."],"tags":["figuratively","informal"]},{"glosses":["Unskillful or unartful behavior, particularly when showy and superficially pleasing.","An act of tinkering, playing around, or fidgeting with something."],"tags":["figuratively","informal"]},{"glosses":["Any rail or device that prevents items from sliding off a table, stove, etc. in rough water."],"tags":["especially"],"topics":["nautical","transport"]},{"glosses":["An arrest warrant."],"tags":["UK","obsolete","slang"]},{"glosses":["A watchman's rattle."],"tags":["UK","obsolete","slang"]},{"examples":[{"text":"Done at a fiddle.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A trifling amount."],"tags":["UK","obsolete","slang"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/ˈfɪd(ə)l/","tags":["Received-Pronunciation"]},{"enpr":"fĭdʹl","tags":["General-American"]},{"ipa":"/ˈfɪdl̩/","tags":["General-American"]},{"ipa":"[ˈfɪɾl̩]","tags":["General-American"]}],"upstream_line":220768,"upstream_offset":1070770658,"upstream_record_sha256":"10b7da4c7cf312fb5e924ea9e3f36c759b648d87e92b2c77282e16889fc69e3c","word":"fiddle"}
